### [1] [Domain-level metacognitive monitoring in frontier LLMs: A 33-model atlas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06673)
*Jon-Paul Cacioli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对33个前沿LLM在MMLU六域上的元认知监控研究（47,151观测值）发现，聚合指标掩盖了显著的域级变异：应用/专业知识最易监控，自然科学和形式推理最难监控，六域分组仅为实用基准分类而非潜在结构，且探针格式具有特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合元认知质量分数掩盖了MMLU基准域内的模型变异，需要在域级别探索LLM的元认知监控能力差异。

Method: 对来自8个模型家族的33个前沿LLM进行测试，每个模型在6个MMLU域（每域250题）上作答，使用口头置信度（0-100）计算每个模型-域单元的Type-2 AUROC，同时进行主体层面一致性分析、族内配置形状聚类分析、探针格式特异性验证。共47,151个观测值。

Result: 所有高于随机水平的聚合监控模型都显示出非平凡的域级变异：应用/专业知识域最易监控（平均AUROC=.742，21/33模型排名前2），形式推理和自然科学最难（27/33模型排名后2）；中间三域统计上无法区分（Kendall's W=.164）。族内配置形状聚类在Anthropic、Google-Gemini、Qwen显著（p<.0001）但DeepSeek、Google-Gemma、OpenAI不显著。Bootstrap 95%CI中位宽度.199，聚合分半稳定性r=.893，配置级分半稳定性较弱（总中位数r=.184）。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，聚合指标会掩盖基准域的稳定变异性，支持在特定应用领域部署前进行基准阶段域筛选。6域分组是务实的基准分类法而非经验证的潜在结构，不同模型家族在配置形状聚类上存在差异，探针格式特异性得到验证。

Abstract: Aggregate metacognitive quality scores mask within-model variation across MMLU benchmark domains. We administered 1,500 MMLU items (250 per domain, under an a priori six-domain grouping) to 33 frontier LLMs from eight model families and computed Type-2 AUROC per model-domain cell using verbalized confidence (0-100). Total observations: 47,151. Every model with above-chance aggregate monitoring showed non-trivial domain-level variation. Applied/Professional knowledge was reliably the easiest benchmark domain to monitor (mean AUROC = .742, ranked top-2 in 21 of 33 models); Formal Reasoning and Natural Science were reliably the hardest (one of the two ranked bottom-2 in 27 of 33 models). The three middle domains were statistically indistinguishable (Kendall's W = .164). A subject-level coherence analysis (within-domain similarity ratio = 0.95) confirms the six-domain grouping is a pragmatic benchmark taxonomy, not a validated latent construct. Within-family profile-shape clustering is significant for Anthropic, Google-Gemini, and Qwen (permutation p < .0001) but not DeepSeek, Google-Gemma, or OpenAI. Gemma 4 31B showed a +.202 AUROC improvement over Gemma 3 27B. Three models classified Invalid on binary KEEP/WITHDRAW probes produced normal profiles under verbalized confidence, confirming probe-format specificity. Bootstrap 95% CIs on 198 cells have median width .199. Split-half aggregate stability r = .893; profile-level split-half is weaker (grand median r = .184). These results show stable benchmark-domain variation obscured by aggregate metrics, and support benchmark-stage domain screening as a step before deployment in specific application areas.

</details>


### [2] [VITA-QinYu: Expressive Spoken Language Model for Role-Playing and Singing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06765)
*Jiacheng Xu,Heting Gao,Liufei Xie,Zhenchuan Yang,Lijiang Li,Yiting Chen,Bin Zhang,Meng Chen,Chaoyu Fu,Weifeng Zhao,Wenjiang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VITA-QinYu是首个端到端口语语言模型，支持自然对话、角色扮演和歌唱三种表达模式。采用混合语音-文本范式结合多码本音频令牌实现丰富表达性，通过15.8K小时合成数据训练。在角色扮演和歌唱任务上显著超越同类模型，同时保持最先进的对话能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类语音传达超越语言内容的表达性，包括个性、情绪或表演元素（如安慰性语调或哼唱歌曲），这些被形式化为角色扮演和歌唱。当前的口语语言模型（SLM）仅支持自然对话，缺乏对这些表达性功能的支持。

Method: 采用混合语音-文本范式，扩展了交错式文本-音频建模，使用多码本音频令牌，设计实现了更丰富的副语言表示，同时保持模态间的清晰分离以避免干扰。开发了综合数据生成流程，合成总计15.8K小时的数据用于训练。

Result: 在角色扮演客观基准上超越同类SLM 7个百分点；在5分制MOS歌唱评分上超越同类模型0.13分；在C3和URO对话基准上分别超越先前SLM 1.38和4.98个百分点，达到最先进的对话准确性和流畅性。

Conclusion: VITA-QinYu是首个支持角色扮演和歌唱生成的端到端表达性口语语言模型。代码和模型已开源，提供流式和全双工交互的完整演示支持。

Abstract: Human speech conveys expressiveness beyond linguistic content, including personality, mood, or performance elements, such as a comforting tone or humming a song, which we formalize as role-playing and singing. We present VITA-QinYu, the first expressive end-to-end (E2E) spoken language model (SLM) that goes beyond natural conversation to support both role-playing and singing generation. VITA-QinYu adopts a hybrid speech-text paradigm that extends interleaved text-audio modeling with multi-codebook audio tokens, a design enabling richer paralinguistic representation while preserving a clear separation between modalities to avoid interference. We further develop a comprehensive data generation pipeline to synthesize a total of 15.8K hours of natural conversation, role-playing, and singing data for training. VITA-QinYu demonstrates superior expressiveness, outperforming peer SLMs by 7 percentage points on objective role-playing benchmarks, and surpassing peer models by 0.13 points on a 5-point MOS scale for singing. Simultaneously, it achieves state-of-the-art conversational accuracy and fluency, exceeding prior SLMs by 1.38 and 4.98 percentage points on the C3 and URO benchmarks, respectively. We open-source our code and models and provide an easy-to-use demo with full-stack support for streaming and full-duplex interaction.

</details>


### [3] [IntentGrasp: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Intent Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06832)
*Yuwei Yin,Chuyuan Li,Giuseppe Carenini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IntentGrasp是一个用于评估大语言模型(LLM)意图理解能力的综合基准测试，基于49个高质量开源语料库构建，涵盖12个领域，包含262,759条训练样本和两个评估集。当前主流模型在该基准上表现不佳(All Set低于60%，Gem Set低于25%)，且提出的意图微调(IFT)方法能显著提升模型性能，提升超过30个F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM助手在理解用户意图方面仍有明显不足，缺乏专门的基准来系统评估和改进这一关键能力。

Method: 通过语料库筛选、意图标签情境化和任务格式统一三个步骤构建基准；提出意图微调(IFT)方法对模型进行微调训练。

Result: 20个LLM在基准上表现欠佳，17/20模型在Gem Set上不如随机猜测(15.2%)；IFT方法在All Set上提升30+ F1分数，在Gem Set上提升20+分数；留一域实验证明IFT具有强跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 意图理解是LLM发展的关键方向，IFT是提升该能力的有效途径，对构建更智能、安全的AI助手具有重要意义。

Abstract: Accurately understanding the intent behind speech, conversation, and writing is crucial to the development of helpful Large Language Model (LLM) assistants. This paper introduces IntentGrasp, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating the intent understanding capability of LLMs. Derived from 49 high-quality, open-licensed corpora spanning 12 diverse domains, IntentGrasp is constructed through source datasets curation, intent label contextualization, and task format unification. IntentGrasp contains a large-scale training set of 262,759 instances and two evaluation sets: an All Set of 12,909 test cases and a more balanced and challenging Gem Set of 470 cases. Extensive evaluations on 20 LLMs across 7 families (including frontier models such as GPT-5.4, Gemini-3.1-Pro, and Claude-Opus-4.7) demonstrate unsatisfactory performance, with scores below 60% on All Set and below 25% on Gem set. Notably, 17 out of 20 tested models perform worse than a random-guess baseline (15.2%) on Gem Set, while the estimated human performance is ~81.1%, showing substantial room for improvement. To enhance such ability, this paper proposes Intentional Fine-Tuning (IFT), which fine-tunes the models on the training set in IntentGrasp, yielding significant gains of 30+ F1 points on All Set and 20+ points on Gem Set. Tellingly, the leave-one-domain-out (Lodo) experiments further demonstrate the strong cross-domain generalizability of IFT, verifying that it is a promising approach to substantially enhancing the intent understanding of LLMs. Overall, by benchmarking and boosting intent understanding ability, this study sheds light on a promising path towards more intentional, capable, and safe AI assistants for human benefits and social good.

</details>


### [4] [TajPersLexon: A Tajik-Persian Lexical Resource and Hybrid Model for Cross-Script Low-Resource NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06886)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布了包含40,112词对的Tajik-Persian平行词汇资源TajPersLexon，用于低资源跨脚本检索场景。实验表明神经和检索方法可达到98-99%准确率，且轻量级混合模型在OCR纠正任务中以96.4%准确率提供了最佳的准确率-效率权衡，验证了该任务的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tajik语和Persian语之间的跨脚本词汇检索、对齐和OCR后纠正在实际应用中具有重要价值，但现有大型多语言模型在此低资源跨脚本场景下表现不佳。

Method: 采用三类方法进行基准测试：（i）轻量级混合管道，（ii）神经序列到序列模型，（iii）检索方法。所有实验在CPU-only环境下进行，使用固定随机种子以确保完全可复现。

Result: 神经和检索基线达到98-99%的top-1准确率；混合模型在OCR后纠正任务中达到96.4%的准确率，且具有更好的效率表现。

Conclusion: TajPersLexon在低资源跨脚本词汇匹配任务中是可行的；混合模型在准确率和效率之间取得了最佳平衡（OCR任务达96.4%）；大型多语言句子Transformer在此任务上反而失败；代码和数据将公开以确保可复现性。

Abstract: This work introduces TajPersLexon, a curated Tajik--Persian parallel lexical resource of 40,112 word and short-phrase pairs for cross-script lexical retrieval, transliteration, and alignment in low-resource settings. We conduct a comprehensive CPU-only benchmark comparing three methodological families: (i) a lightweight hybrid pipeline, (ii) neural sequence-to-sequence models, and (iii) retrieval methods. Our evaluation establishes that the task is essentially solvable, with neural and retrieval baselines achieving 98-99% top-1 accuracy. Crucially, we demonstrate that while large multilingual sentence transformers fail on this exact lexical matching, our interpretable hybrid model offers a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off for practical applications, achieving 96.4% accuracy in an OCR post-correction task. All experiments use fixed random seeds for full reproducibility. The dataset, code, and models will be publicly released.

</details>


### [5] [MIST: Multimodal Interactive Speech-based Tool-calling Conversational Assistants for Smart Homes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06897)
*Maximillian Chen,Xuanming Zhang,Michael Peng,Zhou Yu,Alexandros Papangelis,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MIST，一个用于评估多模态大语言模型在语音驱动IoT设备控制任务上的合成数据集，发现当前模型在该领域存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界IoT设备的语音控制面临独特挑战：需同时处理时空约束、语音输入、动态状态追踪和混合主动交互模式，但现有研究对此关注不足。

Method: 构建了MIST数据集——一个多轮、语音驱动的IoT代码生成任务，并开发了可扩展的数据生成框架。

Result: 开闭源多模态LLM之间存在显著差距，即使是前沿闭源LLM仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: MIST及数据生成框架的发布将推动物理世界约束推理的混合主动语音助手研究。

Abstract: The rise of Internet of Things (IoT) devices in the physical world necessitates voice-based interfaces capable of handling complex user experiences. While modern Large Language Models (LLMs) already demonstrate strong tool-usage capabilities, modeling real-world IoT devices presents a difficult, understudied challenge which combines modeling spatiotemporal constraints with speech inputs, dynamic state tracking, and mixed-initiative interaction patterns. We introduce MIST (the Multimodal Interactive Speech-based Tool-calling Dataset), a synthetic multi-turn, voice-driven code generation task that operates over IoT devices. We find that there is a significant gap between open- and closed-weight multimodal LLMs on MIST, and that even frontier closed-weight LLMs have substantial headroom. We release MIST and an extensible data generation framework to build related datasets in order to facilitate research on mixed-initiative voice assistants which reason about physical world constraints.

</details>


### [6] [Cognitive Agent Compilation for Explicit Problem Solver Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07040)
*Hyeongdon Moon,Carolyn Rosé,John Stamper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出认知智能体编译(CAC)框架，利用强教师LLM将问题解决知识编译为显式目标智能体，以解决通用LLM在教育场景中缺乏可解释性和可控性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM因广泛预训练而难以约束，不适合需要可控性的教育场景。教育系统需要可检查和可编辑的知识状态：教育者需了解系统假设学习者掌握的知识，学习者需系统能基于显式技能、错误概念和策略进行推理。

Method: 受认知架构启发，提出CAC框架，将(i)知识表示、(ii)问题解决策略、(iii)验证与更新规则三者分离。使用强教师LLM将问题解决知识编译为显式目标智能体。使用小型语言模型实现早期概念验证。

Result: 展示了关键设计权衡，特别是在显式控制与可扩展泛化之间的矛盾。证明了使用小型语言模型进行概念验证的可行性。

Conclusion: CAC作为迈向教育应用边界知识AI的初步尝试，旨在使有限问题解决在教育场景中更具可检查性和可编辑性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for tutoring, feedback generation, and content creation, but their broad pretraining makes them hard to constrain and poor substitutes for controllable learners. Educational systems often require inspectable and editable knowledge states: educators want to know what a system assumes the learner knows, and learners benefit when the system can justify actions in terms of explicit skills, misconceptions, and strategies. Inspired by cognitive architectures, we propose Cognitive Agent Compilation (CAC), a framework that uses a strong teacher LLM to compile problem-solving knowledge into an explicit target agent. CAC separates (i) knowledge representation, (ii) problem-solving policy, and (iii) verification and update rules, with the goal of making bounded problem solving more inspectable and editable in educational settings. We present an early proof of concept implemented with Small Language Models that surfaces key design trade-offs, particularly between explicit control and scalable generalization, and positions CAC as an initial step toward bounded-knowledge AI for educational applications.

</details>


### [7] [Reflections and New Directions for Human-Centered Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06901)
*Caleb Ziems,Dora Zhao,Rose E. Wang,Matthew Jörke,Ahmad Rushdi,Advit Deepak,Sunny Yu,Anshika Agarwal,Harshvardhan Agarwal,Gabriela Aranguiz-Dias,Aditri Bhagirath,Justine Breuch,Huanxing Chen,Ruishi Chen,Sarah Chen,Haocheng Fan,William Fang,Cat Gonzales Fergesen,Daniel Frees,Tian Gao,Ziqing Huang,Vishal Jain,Yucheng Jiang,Kirill Kalinin,Su Doga Karaca,Arpandeep Khatua,Teland La,Isabelle Levent,Miranda Li,Xinling Li,Yongce Li,Angela Liu,Minsik Oh,Nathan J. Paek,Anthony Qin,Emily Redmond,Michael J. Ryan,Aadesh Salecha,Xiaoxian Shen,Pranava Singhal,Shashanka Subrahmanya,Mei Tan,Irawadee Thawornbut,Michelle Vinocour,Xiaoyue Wang,Zheng Wang,Henry Jin Weng,Pawan Wirawarn,Shirley Wu,Sophie Wu,Yichen Xie,Patrick Ye,Sean Zhang,Yutong Zhang,Cathy Zhou,Yiling Zhao,James Landay,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个"以人为中心的大型语言模型"(HCLLMs)开发框架，整合了NLP、HCI和负责任AI的视角，强调在模型开发的每个阶段（从系统设计到部署）都应认真对待人类关切、偏好、价值观和目标，而不仅仅是在后期训练阶段简单处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在商业、教育、金融、医疗、法律、科学等领域的全球影响力日益增强，单纯追求技术能力已不足够，必须在模型开发的每个阶段将人类优先事项纳入考量。

Method: 整合NLP、HCI和负责任AI三个领域的研究视角，提出覆盖整个开发流程的以人为中心的框架，涵盖系统设计、数据获取、模型训练、评估和负责任部署等各个阶段。

Result: 提供了一套覆盖模型开发生命周期各阶段的以人为中心的洞察和建议。

Conclusion: 通过一个关于HCLLMs与未来工作关系的案例研究，展示了该框架的应用价值和实际意义。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly shaping the private and professional lives of users, with numerous applications in business, education, finance, healthcare, law, and science. With this rise in global influence comes greater urgency to build, evaluate, and deploy these systems in a manner that prioritizes not only technical capabilities but also human priorities. This work presents a framework for developing Human-Centered Large Language Models (HCLLMs), which integrates perspectives from Natural Language Processing (NLP), Human-Computer Interaction (HCI), and responsible AI. Considering the ethics, economics, and technical objectives of language modeling, we argue that model developers need to address human concerns, preferences, values, and goals, not only during a cursory post-training stage, but rather with rigor and care at every stage of the pipeline. This paper offers human-centered insights and recommendations for developers at each stage, from system design to data sourcing, model training, evaluation, and responsible deployment. Then we conclude with a case study, applying these insights to understand the future of work with HCLLMs.

</details>


### [8] [MELD: Multi-Task Equilibrated Learning Detector for AI-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06903)
*Chenjun Li,Cheng Wan,Johannes C. Paetzold*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MELD是一个用于AI生成文本检测的多任务均衡学习框架，通过在主检测任务上附加生成器家族、攻击类型和源域的辅助头来学习更丰富的表示，并使用EMA教师-攻击增强学生蒸馏和硬负样本排序损失提升鲁棒性。推理时仅保留主检测头，接口与普通检测器相同。在RAID排行榜上表现最强，在自建评估集MELD-eval上达到99.9% TPR（1% FPR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测器多针对单一AI/Human二分类目标优化，当该目标趋于饱和时，模型缺乏动力学习生成器、攻击类型或领域结构。这导致检测器在面对对抗攻击、跨生成器迁移、以及低假阳性率场景下性能急剧下降，无法满足实际部署需求。

Method: MELD采用多任务学习架构：共享编码器连接四个输出头（主检测头+生成器家族/攻击类型/源域三个辅助头），通过学习到的同方差不确定性权重平衡四项损失。训练策略包括：(1) EMA教师在干净输入上预测，攻击增强的学生模型蒸馏至教师；(2) 硬负样本成对排序损失扩大AI文本与易混淆人类文本的分数间距。推理时丢弃所有辅助头，仅保留主检测头。

Result: 在公开RAID排行榜上，MELD是最强的开源检测器，与领先的商业模型具有竞争力，尤其在对抗攻击和低FPR场景下优势明显。在标准保留基准和自建的MELD-eval（新发布聊天模型的评估池）上，MELD无需额外微调即可达到99.9% TPR（1% FPR），而多数基线方法显著退化。

Conclusion: 通过多任务辅助监督学习生成器、攻击和领域结构，结合蒸馏和硬负样本优化，MELD实现了鲁棒、通用且低FPR的AI文本检测。移除辅助头后保持轻量接口，兼具高性能与实用性。

Abstract: Large language models are now embedded in everyday writing workflows, making reliable AI-generated text detection important for academic integrity, content moderation, and provenance tracking. In practice, however, a detector must do more than achieve high aggregate AUROC on clean, in-distribution human and AI text: it should remain robust to attacks and adversarial rewrites, transfer to unseen generators and domains, and operate at low false-positive rates (FPR). Most existing detectors optimize a single AI/Human objective, giving the representation little incentive to learn generator, attack, or domain structure once the binary task saturates. We introduce MELD (Multi-Task Equilibrated Learning Detector), a deployable detector for AI-generated text that enriches binary detection with auxiliary supervision. MELD attaches generator-family, attack-type, and source-domain heads to a shared encoder, and balances the four losses with learned homoscedastic uncertainty weights. To improve robustness, an EMA teacher predicts on clean inputs while an attack-augmented student is distilled toward the teacher. MELD further uses a hard-negative pairwise ranking loss to enlarge the score margin between AI-generated texts and the most confusable human texts. At inference, all auxiliary heads are discarded, giving MELD the same interface and cost as a standard detector. On the public RAID leaderboard, MELD is the strongest open-source detector and is competitive with leading commercial models, especially under attack and at low FPR. Across standard held-out benchmarks, MELD matches or outperforms supervised baselines. We further introduce MELD-eval, a held-out evaluation pool built from recent chat models released by four major LLM providers. Without additional finetuning, MELD achieves 99.9% TPR at 1% FPR on MELD-eval, while many baselines degrade sharply.

</details>


### [9] [Can LLMs Take Retrieved Information with a Grain of Salt?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06919)
*Behzad Shayegh,Mohamed Osama Ahmed,Fred Tung,Leo Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM难以根据检索信息确定性调整回答的问题，评估8个模型发现三类系统性缺陷，通过交互策略组合在不修改模型权重的情况下将错误率降低25%，证明交互设计可有效提升模型可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在医疗、金融等高风险领域应用时，难以根据检索信息的确定性程度适当调整响应，存在严重后果。

Method: 评估8个LLM的情境确定性服从性，提出包含先验提醒、确定性重校准、情境简化的交互策略组合。

Result: 该方法平均降低25%的服从性错误。

Conclusion: 交互设计可有效提升LLM在不确定性场景下的可靠性，且该策略可跨模型迁移，无需修改模型权重。

Abstract: Large language models have demonstrated impressive retrieval-augmented capabilities. However, a crucial area remains underexplored: their ability to appropriately adapt responses to the certainty of the retrieved information. It is a limitation with real consequences in high-stakes domains like medicine and finance. We evaluate eight LLMs on their context-certainty obedience, measuring how well they adjust responses to match expressed context certainty. Our analysis reveals systematic limitations: LLMs struggle to recall prior knowledge after observing an uncertain context, misinterpret expressed certainties, and overtrust complex contexts. To address these, we propose an interaction strategy combining prior reminders, certainty recalibration, and context simplification. This approach reduces obedience errors by 25% on average, without modifying model weights, demonstrating the efficacy of interaction design in enhancing LLM reliability. Our contributions include a principled evaluation metric, empirical insights into LLMs' uncertainty handling, and a portable strategy to improve context-certainty obedience across diverse LLMs.

</details>


### [10] [MultiSoc-4D: A Benchmark for Diagnosing Instruction-Induced Label Collapse in Closed-Set LLM Annotation of Bengali Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06940)
*Souvik Pramanik,S. M. Riaz Rahman Antu,Shak Mohammad Abyad,Md. Ibrahim Khalil,Md. Shahriar Hussain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究介绍了MultiSoc-4D——一个孟加拉语社交媒体数据集基准，包含58K+条来自六个平台的评论，采用四维度标注（类别、情感、仇恨言论、讽刺）。研究揭示了LLM在低资源语言标注中存在严重的标签崩塌问题：偏好默认标签导致高假阳性一致率和大量漏检，讽刺检测一致性接近随机水平。该问题在40+个不同架构的LLM中普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM自动标注是扩展NLP数据集的核心方法，但针对低资源语言的闭集指令场景下，LLM的行为特征尚未得到充分研究。该研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用结构化标注pipeline，由ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude、Grok四个LLM分别标注不同数据分区，同时共享20%的公共验证集。通过Fleiss' Kappa统计验证标注一致性，系统性诊断LLM行为。

Result: 发现LLM存在"指令诱导的标签崩塌"现象——系统性偏好回退标签（Other、Neutral、No），导致标注一致率虚高但漏检大量少数类别。相比人工校准参考，LLM分别漏检79%和75%的仇恨言论和讽刺内容。讽刺检测的Fleiss' Kappa近乎为零（κ≈-0.001），验证了这是一种"标签一致性幻觉"。在40+个LLM中的基准测试表明，该偏差在不同架构中普遍存在。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了LLM在低资源语言标注任务中存在系统性的标注偏差问题，提出了"指令诱导的标签崩塌"现象，发现该问题在不同架构的40+个LLM中普遍存在。MultiSoc-4D数据集的发布为Bengali NLP社区提供了重要的诊断基准资源。

Abstract: Annotation automation via Large Language Models (LLMs) is the core approach for scaling NLP datasets; however, LLM behavior with respect to closed-set instructions in low-resource languages has not been well studied. We present MultiSoc-4D, a Bengali social media dataset benchmark, which contains 58K+ social media comments from six sources annotated along four dimensions: category, sentiment, hate speech, and sarcasm. By employing a structured pipeline where ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude, and Grok individually annotate separate partitions, while sharing a common validation set of 20%, we diagnose LLM behavior systematically. We discover a prevalent phenomenon called "instruction-induced label collapse", wherein LLMs show a systematic preference towards fallback labels (Other, Neutral, No), leading to high agreement rates but under-detection of minority categories. For example, we find that LLMs failed to detect 79% and 75% of instances with hateful and sarcastic content compared to a human-calibrated reference. Furthermore, we prove that it represents a "label agreement illusion", statistically validated via almost null Fleiss' Kappa ($κ\approx -0.001$) on sarcasm detection. Across 40+ LLMs, we benchmark this annotation bias propagation within the training pipeline, regardless of architectural differences. We release MultiSoc-4D as a diagnostic benchmark for annotation biases in Bengali NLP.

</details>


### [11] [Group of Skills: Group-Structured Skill Retrieval for Agent Skill Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06978)
*Kun Zeng,Yu Huo,Siyu Zhang,Zi Ye,Yuecheng Zhuo,Haoyue Liu,Yuquan Lu,Junhao Wen,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GoSkills通过将技能检索对象从扁平技能列表转变为带角色标签的执行上下文，解决现有技能检索方法中技能内部角色不明确的问题。该方法构建以锚点为中心的技能组，通过组图扩展支持组，将选定组计划压缩为有限的原子技能负载，并生成包含Start、Support、Check、Avoid字段的固定执行契约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能增强型智能体依赖大型可重用技能库，但传统检索方法返回原子技能或依赖感知的捆绑包，其内部角色不明确。智能体需要自行推断执行入口、支持技能、可见需求和故障规避指导，这增加了使用复杂性和出错风险。

Method: GoSkills是一种推理时组结构化检索方法，包含四个核心步骤：1)从类型化技能图构建以锚点为中心的技能组；2)通过组图扩展支持组；3)将选定组计划瓶颈化为有限的原子技能负载；4)渲染包含Start、Support、Check、Avoid字段的固定执行契约。该方法无需更改下游智能体、技能负载或执行环境。

Result: 在SkillsBench和ALFWorld基准上的实验表明：GoSkills在小技能预算下保持可见需求覆盖；优于扁平技能访问基线；相比结构化检索参考方法，通常能提升奖励和智能体单独运行时间。

Conclusion: GoSkills有效解决了技能检索中的角色隐式问题，通过组结构化检索和执行契约渲染，为智能体提供了清晰的技能执行上下文，在保持系统兼容性的同时提升了任务性能和运行效率。

Abstract: Skill-augmented agents increasingly rely on large reusable skill libraries, but retrieving relevant skills is not the same as presenting usable context. Existing methods typically return atomic skills or dependency-aware bundles whose internal roles remain implicit, leaving the agent to infer the execution entry point, support skills, visible requirements, and failure-avoidance guidance. We introduce Group of Skills (GoSkills), an inference-time group-structured retrieval method that changes the agent-facing retrieval object from a flat skill list to a compact, role-labeled execution context. GoSkills builds anchor-centered skill groups from a typed skill graph, expands support groups through a group graph, bottlenecks the selected group plan into a bounded set of atomic skill payloads, and renders a fixed execution contract with Start, Support, Check, and Avoid fields, without changing the downstream agent, skill payloads, or execution environment. Experiments on SkillsBench and ALFWorld show that GoSkills preserves visible-requirement coverage under a small skill budget, improves over flat skill-access baselines, and often improves reward and agent-only runtime relative to structural retrieval references.

</details>


### [12] [Towards Closing the Autoregressive Gap in Language Modeling via Entropy-Gated Continuous Bitstream Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07013)
*Georgios Batzolis,Mark Girolami,Luca Ambrogioni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: The paper proposes bitstream diffusion language modeling, representing text as continuous binary bitstreams with entropy-rate gated stochastic sampling, achieving state-of-the-art results among diffusion language models while eliminating the O(V) vocabulary scaling bottleneck.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion language models (DLMs) offer parallel, order-agnostic generation but historically lag autoregressive models in sample quality and diversity. While recent continuous flow approaches over token embeddings have narrowed this gap, the paper seeks to further close the autoregressive gap through a novel representation.

Method: 1) Represent semantic tokens as fixed-width analog bit sequences; 2) Matched-filter residual parameterization to isolate contextual learning from analytic independent-bit posteriors; 3) Stochastic sampler applying Langevin-type corrections gated by entropy-rate profile

Result: On LM1B (130M parameters): GenPPL=59.76 at entropy 4.31 using 256 NFEs, outperforming prior DLM baselines and reaching autoregressive reference. On OWT: GenPPL=27.06 at entropy 5.26 using 4× fewer steps than previous 1024-NFE baselines, establishing a new continuous-DLM Pareto frontier.

Conclusion: Bitstream diffusion represents text as binary bitstreams with a matched-filter residual parameterization, using a stochastic sampler with Langevin-type entropy-rate gated corrections. This approach removes the O(V) vocabulary scaling bottleneck by predicting O(log V) bitwise logits via semantic bit-patching.

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) promise parallel, order-agnostic generation, but on standard benchmarks they have historically lagged behind autoregressive models in sample quality and diversity. Recent continuous flow and diffusion approaches over token embeddings have narrowed this gap, suggesting continuous state spaces are highly effective for language. In this work, we further close the autoregressive gap by modeling text as a continuous diffusion process over fixed-width binary bitstreams. Our approach represents semantic tokens as analog bit sequences and utilizes a matched-filter residual parameterization to isolate contextual learning from analytic independent-bit posteriors. Crucially, we adopt a stochastic sampler that applies Langevin-type corrections gated by the entropy-rate profile, automatically concentrating stochasticity in high-information regions while remaining nearly deterministic elsewhere. On the One Billion Word Benchmark (LM1B), our 130M-parameter bitstream model reaches a generative perplexity ($\GenPPL$) of $59.76$ at matched real-data entropy ($4.31$) using 256 neural function evaluations (NFEs), decisively outperforming prior DLM baselines and reaching the autoregressive reference. On OpenWebText (OWT), our stochastic sampler establishes a new continuous-DLM Pareto frontier, achieving $\GenPPL=27.06$ at an entropy of $5.26$ using $4\times$ fewer steps than previous 1024-NFE baselines. As an additional architectural benefit, bitstream diffusion removes the $\mathcal{O}(V)$ vocabulary scaling bottleneck shared by standard DLMs. By predicting $\mathcal{O}(\log V)$ bitwise logits via semantic bit-patching, our model yields a reduced memory footprint and higher throughput, demonstrating a scalable paradigm for language generation as vocabulary sizes grow.

</details>


### [13] [TCMIIES: A Browser-Based LLM-Powered Intelligent Information Extraction System for Academic Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07507)
*Hanqing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TCMIIES是一个基于浏览器的零安装平台，利用商业LLM API从学术文献中进行结构化信息提取。该系统通过图形界面让研究人员无需编程即可定义自定义提取模式，同时确保数据隐私（纯前端架构本地处理），支持五个主要LLM提供商，实现并发批处理和自动重试机制。评估表明，在中医药研究中结构化输出合规率超过94%，信息提取准确率与领域专家标注相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术出版物呈指数级增长，现有解决方案存在以下问题：需要专用基础设施、编程专业知识、或需微调的领域特定模型，给专业领域研究人员设置了较高门槛。需要一种既易于使用又能保护隐私且成本效益高的自动化工具来从非结构化科学文本中提取结构化知识。

Method: 1) 纯前端架构实现本地隐私保护；2) 创新性的模式引导提示框架，支持自动系统提示生成；3) 直观的图形界面让用户无需编程即可定义自定义提取模式；4) 支持五大LLM提供商；5) 实现并发批处理与自动重试机制；6) 针对CNKI、万方等中文学术数据库提供智能字段映射功能。

Result: 在中医药研究多个提取场景的综合评估中，结构化输出合规率超过94%，信息提取准确率与领域专家标注水平相当。

Conclusion: TCMIIES为专业领域（尤其是中医药研究）的学术信息提取提供了一个实用、可访问的解决方案，有效弥合了先进LLM能力与领域特定学术信息提取需求之间的差距。该系统具有灵活性、隐私保护性和成本效益，特别适合需要定制化提取工具的专业研究人员。

Abstract: The exponential growth of academic publications has created an urgent need for automated tools capable of extracting structured knowledge from unstructured scientific texts. While large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in natural language understanding and information extraction, existing solutions often require specialized infrastructure, programming expertise, or fine-tuned domain-specific models that create barriers for researchers in specialized fields. This paper presents TCMIIES, a browser-based, zero-installation platform that leverages commercial LLM APIs to perform structured information extraction from academic literature. The system employs a novel schema-guided prompting framework with automatic system prompt generation, enabling researchers to define custom extraction schemas through an intuitive graphical interface without any programming. TCMIIES features a pure front-end architecture that ensures data privacy by processing all information locally in the browser, supports five major LLM providers, implements concurrent batch processing with automatic retry mechanisms, and provides intelligent field mapping for Chinese academic databases including CNKI and Wanfang. We demonstrate the system's effectiveness through comprehensive evaluation across multiple extraction scenarios in Traditional Chinese Medicine research, achieving structured output compliance rates exceeding 94\% and information extraction accuracy comparable to domain-expert annotation. The system represents a practical, accessible solution that bridges the gap between advanced LLM capabilities and domain-specific academic information extraction needs, particularly for researchers in specialized fields who require flexible, privacy-preserving, and cost-effective extraction tools.

</details>


### [14] [MedExAgent: Training LLM Agents to Ask, Examine, and Diagnose in Noisy Clinical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07058)
*Yicheng Gao,Xiaolin Zhou,Yahan Li,Yue Zhao,Ruishan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文将临床诊断建模为POMDP问题，引入系统性噪声模拟真实临床场景，训练MedExAgent通过两阶段方法（监督微调+DAPO优化）实现与大型模型相当的诊断性能，同时具备成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学LLM基准和自动诊断方法过度简化临床诊断过程，仅关注单轮问答、无噪声对话或顺序检查制作，忽视了临床诊断的交互性和不确定性本质，无法真实反映临床实践中的复杂场景。

Method: 1) 将临床诊断形式化为POMDP框架，定义三种动作类型（提问、医疗检查工具调用、诊断）；2) 设计系统性噪声模型（7种患者噪声+3种检查噪声）；3) 采用两阶段训练：监督微调（基于Calgary-Cambridge临床访谈模型构建的合成对话）+ DAPO优化（组合奖励：诊断准确性、工具调用质量、检查成本包括费用和患者不适度）。

Result: MedExAgent在诊断性能上可与更大的模型相媲美，同时保持了成本效益的检查策略。

Conclusion: MedExAgent在保持成本效益的检查策略的同时，实现了与更大模型相当的诊断性能，验证了将临床诊断建模为POMDP并结合两阶段训练方法的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world clinical diagnosis is a complex process in which the doctor is required to obtain information from both interaction with the patient and conducting medical exams. Additionally, the doctor needs to adapt to different patient personas, as well as noisy and incomplete information that can happen at any time during the process. However, existing benchmarks for medical LLMs and methods for automatic diagnosis largely simplify this process by reducing it to single-turn question answering, noise-free conversations, or sequential exam making, etc., ignoring the interactive and uncertain nature of clinical diagnosis. In this paper, we aim to address this gap by formalizing clinical diagnosis as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) with three action types: questioning the patient, ordering medical exams as tool calls, and issuing a diagnosis. We also introduce a systematic noise model comprising seven patient noise types and three exam noise types. Using our proposed environment, we train an effective diagnosis agent, \textbf{MedExAgent}, through a two-stage pipeline that first performs supervised finetuning on synthetic conversations structured after the Calgary-Cambridge model for clinical interviews, and then applies DAPO to optimize a composite reward capturing diagnostic accuracy, tool call quality, and exam cost including financial cost and patient discomfort. Through extensive experiments and ablation studies, we demonstrate that MedExAgent achieves diagnostic performance comparable to larger models while maintaining cost-efficient examination strategies.

</details>


### [15] [WiCER: Wiki-memory Compile, Evaluate, Refine Iterative Knowledge Compilation for LLM Wiki Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07068)
*Juan M. Huerta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM Wiki模式中的"编译差距"问题，提出了基于CEGAR思想的WiCER迭代算法，通过诊断评估、事实识别和强制保留三个步骤，在1-2次迭代内即可恢复80%的质量损失，灾难性失败率降低55%。实验覆盖17个领域6800个问题，证明了针对性诊断比通用固定策略更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Wiki模式承诺亚秒级延迟和零检索失败的高质量上下文访问，但存在"编译差距"问题：LLM编译将原始文档提炼为wiki时，可能灾难性地丢弃关键事实。需要系统性地表征这一差距并提出解决方案。

Method: WiCER（Wiki-memory Compile, Evaluate, Refine）：一种基于反例引导抽象细化（CEGAR）思想的迭代算法。该方法包含三个核心步骤：1）对编译后的wiki进行诊断探测评估；2）识别被丢弃的关键事实；3）在后续编译中强制保留这些事实。通过1-2次迭代循环实现编译质量的逐步恢复。

Result: 在17个RepLiQA领域（6800个问题）的评估表明：1）全上下文KV缓存推理在精选知识上优于RAG（4.38 vs 4.08分，TTFT快7.3倍）；2）但在大规模下因注意力稀释而性能下降；3）盲目编译完全失败（2.14-2.32分 vs 3.46，灾难性失败率53-60%）；4）WiCER 1-2次迭代恢复80%质量损失，灾难性失败率相对降低55%。

Conclusion: WiCER通过1-2次迭代即可恢复80%的质量损失，将灾难性失败率降低55%。研究表明，针对性诊断（+0.95提升）比通用固定策略（+0.16）更有效，为LLM知识编译提供了可复现的解决方案。

Abstract: The LLM Wiki pattern, to compile and provide domain knowledge into a persistent artifact and serve it to LLMs via KV cache inference, promises context access at sub-second latency with zero retrieval failure. Realizing this requires solving the compilation gap: LLM compilation distilling raw documents into a wiki without catastrophically discarding critical facts. We characterize this gap across 17 RepLiQA domains (6,800 questions): we observe that full context KV cache inference outperforms RAG on curated knowledge (4.38 vs. 4.08 out of 5, 7.3 faster TTFT) but degrades below RAG at scale due to attention dilution, and blind compilation fails entirely (2.14 to 2.32 vs. 3.46, 53 to 60% catastrophic failure rate). To address the compilation gap, we propose WiCER (Wiki-memory Compile, Evaluate, Refine), an iterative algorithm inspired by counterexample-guided abstraction refinement (CEGAR) that closes this gap. WiCER evaluates compiled wikis against diagnostic probes, identifies dropped facts, and forces their preservation in subsequent compilations. One to two iterations recover 80% of lost quality (mean 3.24 vs. 3.47 for raw full-context across the 15 topics with baselines), reducing catastrophic failures by 55% relative. An ablation across all 17 topics confirms that targeted diagnosis (+0.95), not generic pinning (+0.16), drives the gains. All code and benchmarks are released for reproducible research.

</details>


### [16] [Self-Consolidating Language Models: Continual Knowledge Incorporation from Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07076)
*Zekun Wang,Anant Gupta,Zihan Dong,Christopher J. MacLellan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SCoL框架，通过元强化学习让LLM自主学习在给定当前上下文时生成更新指令，选择应更新的Transformer层，实现持续上下文整合，同时限制对已整合信息的干扰。实验证明该方法在知识获取与保留上优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM接收的信息以段落流、对话和长上下文工作流形式递增，但更长的上下文窗口并不等同于有用信息被有效保留和复用。现有方法难以在整合新信息的同时限制对已整合信息的干扰，需要一种持续性的上下文整合机制。

Method: 提出SCoL框架，采用元强化学习在不断演化的模型状态下训练，使LLM能够生成指定哪些Transformer层应被更新的文本更新指令。通过监督式QA奖励（SQuAD）和基于似然的内在奖励（LongBench v2）进行训练。

Result: 相比提示工程、摘要、批量测试时训练和顺序微调基线，SCoL在知识获取与保留上均有提升。分析发现模型倾向于选择高Fisher信息量的层进行更新，说明模型学会了将可塑性路由至损失敏感区域。框架支持从短训练流向长评估流的迁移。

Conclusion: SCoL通过让模型自主学习生成更新指令，实现了高效且低干扰的持续上下文整合。该方法在知识获取与保留两个维度均优于基线方法，展现了良好的可扩展性和跨长度迁移能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly receive information as streams of passages, conversations, and long-context workflows. While longer context windows expose more evidence, they do not ensure that useful information is preserved and reused. We study continual context consolidation: writing current context into model weights while limiting interference with previously consolidated information. We propose \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{Co}nsolidating \textbf{L}anguage Models (SCoL), a post-training framework in which, given current context, an LLM learns to generate textual update instructions specifying which of its own Transformer layers should be updated. Because committed updates change the model that later generates future selections, we train SCoL with meta-reinforcement learning over an evolving model state. We instantiate SCoL with supervised QA rewards on SQuAD knowledge incorporation and intrinsic likelihood-based rewards for LongBench v2 long-context consolidation. Across both settings, SCoL improves acquisition and retention over prompting, summarization, batch test-time training, and sequential finetuning baselines. Analysis of learned selection patterns shows that SCoL encourages the LLM to generate sparse update locations that align with layers of high Fisher information, suggesting that the model learns to route plasticity toward loss-sensitive regions while limiting interference. Moreover, SCoL transfers from shorter meta-training streams to longer LongBench v2 streams at evaluation, suggesting that our framework supports scalable streaming consolidation.

</details>


### [17] [Beyond Single Ground Truth: Reference Monism as Epistemic Injustice in ASR Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07084)
*Anna Seo Gyeong Choi,Maria Teleki,James Caverlee,Miguel del Rio,Corey Miller,Hoon Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了自动语音识别评估中参考单一主义对失语症患者造成的认知不正义问题，通过引入诠释鸿沟概念和EID指标，论证了现行评估框架的系统性偏见，并提出WER-Range作为更具包容性的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ASR评估依赖单一"干净"转录作为标准参考，对失语症患者的临床有意义的不流利表达（如停顿、重复、犹豫等）进行负面评判。这种做法将语言多样性错误地量化为系统误差，导致该群体在技术评估中系统性处于不利地位。

Method: 研究团队构建了哲学理论框架来界定"诠释鸿沟"概念，形式化了"认知不正义距离"(EID)指标以量化参考单一主义的代价，并使用AphasiaBank语料库进行实证研究，比较不同转录约定下的WER表现差异。

Result: 实证研究表明，使用AphasiaBank数据验证了WER结果会因所采用的转录约定不同而产生显著差异，证实了参考单一主义确实会对特定说话人群造成不公正的评估结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为ASR评估提出了一个根本性的反思框架，通过引入"诠释鸿沟"概念和EID指标，揭示了评估基础设施中对特定语言群体的系统性偏见。提出的WER-Range方法旨在推动更具包容性的语音技术评估标准。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) evaluation compares system output to ground truth transcripts, with Word Error Rate (WER) quantifying the distance between them. But ground truth transcripts are not discovered - they are produced by human annotators following conventions that encode normative assumptions about which speech features matter. Different conventions (verbatim, non-verbatim, legal) produce different transcripts of identical speech and judge the same ASR output differently. This paper argues that reference monism - enforcing a single transcription convention as ground truth - commits epistemic injustice. Speakers with aphasia, whose speech includes clinically meaningful disfluencies, are systematically disadvantaged when evaluated against "clean" references that treat those disfluencies as errors. The harm is not merely differential performance, but that evaluative infrastructure lacks interpretive resources to recognize their contributions as legitimate. We develop a philosophical framework introducing the hermeneutical gap, formalize Epistemic Injustice Distance (EID) to measure reference monism's cost, and demonstrate empirically using AphasiaBank that WER varies depending on which convention defines ground truth. We propose WER-Range: reporting performance across legitimate conventions rather than assuming a single correct answer.

</details>

### [18] [Rethinking Priority Scheduling for Sequential Multi-Agent Decision Making in Stackelberg Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07240)
*Xiangyu Liu,Liang Zhang,Bo Jin,Ziqi Wei*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究N层Stackelberg博弈中智能体决策顺序对均衡点的影响，发现改变决策顺序通常导致系统过约束，均衡点随之改变。为此提出层级优先级调整(HPA)方法，通过上层策略动态选择最优决策顺序，下层在时空序贯马尔可夫博弈中执行策略。实验表明该方法在多智能体MuJoCo任务上优于基准算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前N层Stackelberg博弈研究通常采用环境默认的智能体决策顺序，但缺乏对决策顺序是否影响博弈均衡点的形式化分析。这引发了一个核心问题：智能体的决策顺序是否必然影响游戏的最终均衡点？

Method: 1) 形式化分析N层Stackelberg博弈中决策顺序的影响，证明改变决策顺序通常导致过约束系统；2) 提出层级优先级调整(HPA)方法：上层策略根据当前博弈状态动态选择最优决策顺序，下层智能体按选定顺序在时空序贯马尔可夫博弈(STMG)中执行策略；3) 采用慢-快更新机制配合共享内在奖励协调多时间尺度学习。

Result: 在高精度控制任务（包括多智能体MuJoCo环境）上的实验表明，HPA方法优于基准算法，并能鲁棒地适应环境变化。

Conclusion: 优化智能体决策顺序在N层Stackelberg博弈中起着关键作用。HPA方法通过动态调整决策顺序有效提升了多智能体系统的决策性能和适应性。

Abstract: Current research applying N-level Stackelberg Game to multi-agent systems often uses the default decision order of agents provided by the environment. However, this raises the question: does the order of agents necessarily affect the final equilibrium point of the game? To address this, we formally analyze the N-level Stackelberg Game, where changing the order in which agents make decisions typically leads to an overdetermined system. As a result, the equilibrium point shifts unless special structural conditions are satisfied. Based on this analysis, we propose the Hierarchical Priority Adjustment (HPA) method, which adjusts and selects the agents' decision order. At the upper level, an upper policy dynamically selects the optimal decision order of agents based on the current game state. At the lower level, agents execute strategies in the Spatio-Temporal Sequential Markov Game (STMG) according to the selected order. To coordinate learning across time scales, we employ a slow-fast update scheme with shared intrinsic rewards derived from the advantage function of the upper policy. Experimental results on high-precision control tasks, including multi-agent MuJoCo, show that HPA outperforms benchmark algorithms and robustly adapts to changing environments. These results highlight the crucial role of optimizing the agents' decision order in N-level Stackelberg Game.

</details>


### [19] [OrchJail: Jailbreaking Tool-Calling Text-to-Image Agents by Orchestration-Guided Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07414)
*Jianming Chen,Yawen Wang,Junjie Wang,Zhe Liu,Qing Wang,Fanjiang Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: OrchJail是一个针对工具调用型T2I智能体的编排引导模糊测试框架，通过学习成功越狱轨迹及其与提示词的因果关系，引导搜索朝向更容易触发不安全多步工具行为的提示词，在攻击成功率、图像保真度和查询成本方面均优于现有方法，同时对常见防御具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示词层面的越狱技术对工具调用型T2I智能体无效，因为有害输出可能来自工具编排——单独无害的步骤组合成不安全的结果，形成了一个新的安全攻击面。

Method: OrchJail利用高风险工具编排模式：通过学习成功越狱工具调用轨迹及其与提示词措辞的因果关系，直接引导模糊测试搜索更有可能触发不安全多步工具行为的提示词，而非依赖表面级文本扰动。

Result: 实验表明OrchJail在代表性的工具调用型T2I智能体上提高了越狱有效性和效率：更高的攻击成功率、更好的图像保真度、更低的查询成本，同时对常见越狱防御具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 工具编排是一个关键且此前未探索的攻击面，OrchJail为揭示T2I智能体中的安全风险提供了新框架。

Abstract: Tool-calling text-to-image (T2I) agents can plan and execute multi-step tool chains to accomplish complex generation and editing queries. However, this capability introduces a new safety attack surface: harmful outputs may arise from tool orchestration, where individually benign steps combine into unsafe results, making prompt-only jailbreak techniques insufficient. We present OrchJail, an orchestration-guided fuzzing framework for jailbreaking tool-calling T2I agents. Its core idea is to exploit high-risk tool-orchestration patterns: by learning from successful jailbreak tool-calling traces and their causal relationships to prompt wording, OrchJail directly guides the fuzzing search toward prompts that are more likely to trigger unsafe multi-step tool behaviors, rather than relying on surface-level textual perturbations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OrchJail improves jailbreak effectiveness and efficiency across representative toolcalling T2I agents, achieving higher attack success rates, better image fidelity, and lower query costs, while remaining robust against common jailbreak defenses. Our work highlights tool orchestration as a critical, previously unexplored attack surface and provides a novel framework for uncovering safety risks in T2I agents.

</details>


### [20] [Synchronizing Minds through Collective Predictive Coding: A Computational Model of Parent-Infant Homeostatic Co-Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07524)
*Yushi Tsubamoto,Takato Horii*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合POMDP与Metropolis-Hastings Naming Game的计算模型，解释父母-婴儿交互中的潜在状态对齐机制。模型假设母亲通过外部感受观察婴儿而婴儿直接感知自身内部状态，两者通过共享通信变量协调动作。在6×6网格世界中的模拟表明，脑间同步可早于完全共享世界模型的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑间同步（IBS）研究缺乏计算层面的机制解释，特别是两个仅具有局部感知访问权限且内部知识不对称的智能体如何在交互中实现潜在表征对齐。

Method: 将主动内感受推理的POMDP公式与集合预测编码假设衍生的MHNG相结合。在6×6内脏状态网格世界中验证模型，其中母亲通过外部感受信号观察婴儿，婴儿直接感知自身内感受状态；两主体通过共享通信变量达成调节动作共识，接受概率由局部可计算的Metropolis-Hastings概率决定。

Result: MHNG介导的交互在调节婴儿内脏状态方面比单边控制条件更具适应性，两个后验分布迅速对齐。值得注意的是，潜在状态对齐的出现远早于学习生成的生成矩阵的收敛，表明表征同步并不以完全共享的世界模型为先决条件。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一种最小化的父婴稳态协同调节计算模型，整合了主动内感受推理的POMDP框架与源自集合预测编码（ CPC）的MHNG机制。该模型能够解释超扫描研究中报告的脑间同步现象，并为IBS提供了候选的计算基础理论。潜在状态对齐早于完全共享世界模型的收敛这一发现，对理解社会认知和早期发展具有重要意义。

Abstract: Inter-brain synchrony (IBS) observed in real-time dyadic interactions, including parent--infant exchanges, suggests that two agents come to share aligned latent representations through interaction. Yet computational accounts of how such alignment can arise between agents that have only local sensory access and asymmetric internal knowledge remain underdeveloped. We propose a constructive model of parent--infant homeostatic co-regulation that integrates a POMDP formulation of active interoceptive inference with the Metropolis--Hastings Naming Game (MHNG) derived from the Collective Predictive Coding (CPC) hypothesis. In our model, the parent observes the infant only through an exteroceptive signal while the infant directly senses its own interoceptive state; the two agents agree on regulatory actions through a shared communicative variable whose acceptance is determined by a locally computable Metropolis--Hastings probability. The agents are further endowed with asymmetric generative-model knowledge: the parent knows how actions transform visceral states but must learn what the infant's body is communicating, whereas the infant perceives its visceral state directly but must learn how actions affect it. In a $6 \times 6$ visceral-state grid world, MHNG-mediated interaction regulated the infant's visceral state more adaptively than one-sided control conditions, and the two posteriors became rapidly aligned. Notably, this latent-state alignment emerged far earlier than the convergence of the learned generative matrices, indicating that representational synchrony does not presuppose fully shared world models. These results offer a minimal constructive account of latent-state alignment compatible with IBS reported in hyperscanning studies and support CPC as a candidate computational basis for inter-brain alignment.

</details>


### [21] [Emergence of Social Reality of Emotion through a Social Allostasis Model with Dynamic Interpretants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07761)
*Kentaro Nomura,Yushi Tsubamoto,Takato Horii*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过计算模型模拟构造主义情绪理论中社会现实（情绪概念共识）的涌现过程，验证了社区层面情绪概念可通过个体间符号交流和身体控制目标的协同调整而形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造主义情绪理论提出情绪是社会建构的现实，但这一涌现过程缺乏计算验证，需要通过计算模型模拟社区层面情绪概念共识的形成机制。

Method: 构建了一个整合符号涌现、符号解释自由度与主动推理的计算模型。两个智能体接收内感受信号，交换推断出的符号，同时调整各自的身体控制目标和符号解释规则。

Result: 两个智能体的内感受先验偏好和符号概率分布实现收敛，证明以社会共识为基础的社会现实可以在个体互动中涌现。

Conclusion: 研究验证了社区层面的社会现实确实可以通过个体间的符号交流和身体控制目标的协同调整而涌现，证实了构造主义情绪理论的核心主张。实验表明，即使个体具有不同的先验偏好和符号解释，通过社会互动可以形成共享的情绪概念体系。

Abstract: The theory of constructed emotion defines social reality as the community-level consensus on emotion concepts assigned to interoceptive sensations arising from bodily allostasis and social interaction. In this study, we simulate this emergence process using a computational model that integrates symbol emergence with degrees of freedom in symbol interpretation and active inference. Two agents receive interoceptive signals, exchange inferred symbols, and simultaneously adapt their bodily control goals and symbol interpretations to each other. Experimental results show that the interoceptive prior preferences and symbol probability distributions of the two agents converge, confirming the emergence of social reality grounded in social consensus.

</details>

### [22] [EMRGF: A Practitioner Framework for Governance-Driven Enterprise Technology Modernization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06703)
*Harveen Punihani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对企业技术现代化项目高失败率问题，提出真正原因在于治理缺陷而非工程能力不足，并提出EMRGF框架作为解决方案。该框架包含四个模块、五种实施工具和培训师培训制度化模型，经验证可实现开发工作量减少30%、测试周期减少35%、零中断迁移和99.9%数据可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业技术现代化项目以高成本和高失败率著称，而主流解释（工程能力不足）被本文认为是不准确的。作者指出现有框架（ITIL、COBIT、TOGAF等）未能提供集成化、可移植的制度化运营模式，导致治理缺陷成为主要失败原因。

Method: 该研究基于24年跨金融服务业、制造业和零售业的实践经验，提出Enterprise Modernization Reliability and Governance Framework (EMRGF)，包含四个相互锁定的模块：云与遗留系统现代化治理、数据平台可靠性与证据完整性、AI赋能自动化治理、以及任务关键可靠性与根因分析例程，并通过五种实施工具和培训师培训制度化模型进行运营化。

Result: EMRGF框架的规模化应用产生了显著效果：开发工作量减少30%，测试周期减少35%，高数据量 estates 的零中断迁移，任务关键分析管道实现99.9%数据可靠性。

Conclusion: EMRGF是一个从业者开发的治理运营模型，明确对齐美国国家政策指令（NIST CSF 2.0、NIST AI RMF、执行令14028和14110），专为制度化采用设计，无需持续外部依赖即可实现。

Abstract: Enterprise technology modernization programs fail at a documented and costly rate, yet the dominant explanation -- inadequate engineering capability -- is incorrect. The primary failure mode is a governance deficit: the absence of structured, repeatable operating routines for how organizations plan, execute, validate, and hand off complex technology change. Existing frameworks -- ITIL, COBIT, TOGAF, scaled agile methodologies, and cloud provider well-architected frameworks -- address adjacent concerns but do not provide an integrated, portable institutional operating model for controlled modernization across migrations, data platforms, and AI-enabled automation. This article presents the Enterprise Modernization Reliability and Governance Framework (EMRGF), a practitioner-developed governance operating model derived from 24 years of applied delivery experience across financial services, industrial manufacturing, and retail enterprises. EMRGF comprises four interlocking modules -- Cloud and Legacy Modernization Governance, Data Platform Reliability and Evidence Integrity, AI-Enabled Automation Governance, and Mission-Critical Reliability and Root-Cause Routines -- operationalized through five implementation tools and a training-of-trainers institutionalization model. Empirical application at scale has produced a 30% reduction in development effort, a 35% reduction in testing cycles, zero-disruption migrations across high-volume data estates, and 99.9% data reliability in mission-critical analytics pipelines. The framework is explicitly aligned with U.S. national policy mandates including NIST CSF 2.0, NIST AI RMF, and Executive Orders 14028 and 14110, and is designed for institutional adoption without ongoing external dependency.

</details>


### [23] [The Single-File Test: A Longitudinal Public-Interface Evaluation of First-Output LLM Web Generation with Social Reach Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06707)
*Diego Cabezas Palacios*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过"HTML AI Battle"项目，对GPT、Gemini、Grok、Claude四个推理模型家族的HTML生成能力进行了8周观测比较。Claude家族表现最佳，在17个提示中有9个获胜，但较长的推理时间与更高质量无关。模型家族比提示措辞更能预测代码冗长程度，而公共指标不足以预测社交媒体影响力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI模型比较研究存在自定义指令、人格调优和修复提示等变量干扰，缺乏标准化、可复现的公共接口协议下的公平评估方法。

Method: 68个单文件HTML生成样本，17个公开实验，四个模型家族在固定公共接口协议下进行对比。使用人类评分与Gemini LLM作为评判层，评估提示遵循度、功能正确性和UI质量。同时进行两个监督预测分析：实验级别的24小时X平台曝光量模型和生成级别的HTML行数模型。

Result: Claude家族表现最强且最稳定，在人类加权评分中赢得17个提示中的9个。长推理时间与整体质量无正相关。Gemini评判者比人类评估者更宽松，且存在自我偏好偏差。X平台曝光量预测模型较弱（MAE=46,874，R²=-0.377），而HTML行数预测模型表现较好（MAE=135.2，R²=0.576），模型家族基线优于提示感知替代方案。

Conclusion: Claude在HTML生成任务中领先；代码冗长程度主要由模型家族驱动而非提示措辞；公开的技术/音频变量不足以预测24小时X平台触达量。研究受限于观测性设计、公共接口漂移、访问路径差异和单一人类评分者。

Abstract: This paper presents an eight-week observational comparison of 68 single-file HTML generations collected across 17 public experiments in the "HTML AI Battle" project between December 10, 2025 and February 4, 2026. Four reasoning model families, GPT, Gemini, Grok, and Claude, were compared under a fixed public-interface protocol with no custom instructions, no personality tuning, and no repair prompts. Each output was evaluated from a rendered browser video using human scores and a Gemini LLM-as-a-judge layer for prompt adherence, functional correctness, and UI quality, then packaged into a standardized social-media protocol spanning X (Twitter), TikTok, and YouTube. The tracker was also used for two supervised predictive analyses: an experiment-level model for 24-hour X impressions and a generation-level model for HTML verbosity.
  Under this protocol, Claude was the strongest and most consistent family, leading mean performance and winning 9/17 prompts under the primary human weighted score. Longer measured reasoning time was not associated with higher quality overall. Gemini as a judge was significantly more lenient than the human evaluator on functional correctness and overall performance, while stable self-favoring bias remained unresolved. The exploratory X-impressions model remained weak under post-screen cross-validation (MAE = 46,874, R^2 = -0.377), whereas the HTML-lines model performed better, with a model-family-only baseline outperforming prompt-aware alternatives (MAE = 135.2, R^2 = 0.576). Overall, selected pre-publication technical/audio variables were not sufficient to predict 24-hour X reach, while code verbosity was driven much more by model family than by prompt wording. The comparisons remain observational and are limited by public-interface drift, access-path differences, and one primary human scorer.

</details>


### [24] [Agentic Coding Needs Proactivity, Not Just Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06717)
*Nghi D. Q. Bui,Georgios Evangelopoulos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了编码智能体从辅助完成到主动介入的发展趋势，指出当前领域缺乏对"主动性"概念的清晰定义和评估标准，提出以"洞察策略"(insight policy)质量作为核心评估框架，并构建了包含反应式、计划式、情境感知式三个层次的主动性分类体系，以及IDQ、CGS、Learning Lift三个评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前领域缺乏对"主动性"在软件开发中含义的清晰界定，也缺少评判未经请求的智能体行为是否真正有用的标准和指标

Method: 提出三层次主动性分类法（反应式、计划式、情境感知式），对比当代编码智能体与五个实用标准，并设计主动用户模拟协议来评估洞察决策质量、上下文基础评分和学习提升效果

Result: 提出了"洞察策略"作为主动编码智能体的核心评估维度，建立了主动性的三层分类体系，并设计了具体的评估协议和三个量化指标（IDQ、CGS、Learning Lift）

Conclusion: 提出"洞察策略"(insight policy)作为评估主动编码智能体的核心框架，并设计了包含IDQ、CGS、Learning Lift三个指标的主动用户模拟评估协议

Abstract: Coding agents are rapidly changing the landscape of software development, moving from inline completion to autonomous systems that edit repositories, open pull requests, respond to issues, and run scheduled or webhook triggered routines across the development life cycle. The next generation is increasingly described as proactive and long-horizon: agents should notice relevant changes before the developer asks, connect signals across tools, decide when to interrupt, and carry preferences across sessions. Yet the field still lacks a clear account of what proactivity means for software development, how it differs from autonomy, what acceptance criteria proactive long-horizon tasks should satisfy, and which metrics determine whether unsolicited agent behavior is useful rather than merely active. Proactive coding agents should be evaluated by the quality and improvement of their insight policy: the policy that decides what matters next, what evidence supports it, whether to show it, and how to adapt after feedback. This view is grounded in the principles of mixed initiative interaction. We propose a three level taxonomy of proactivity (Reactive, Scheduled, and Situation Aware), compare contemporary coding agents against five practical criteria, and sketch an active user simulation protocol with three evaluation targets: Insight Decision Quality (IDQ), Context Grounding Score (CGS), and Learning Lift

</details>


### [25] [A Self-Healing Framework for Reliable LLM-Based Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06737)
*Cheonsu Jeong,Younggun Shin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可靠性感知的自我修复框架，用于解决LLM驱动软件智能体的不可靠性问题。该框架通过故障检测、可靠性评估和自动化恢复三大机制协同工作，在多智能体工作流环境中验证了其在提升任务成功率、增强系统鲁棒性方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自主智能体在复杂软件系统中应用日益广泛，但面临幻觉、执行错误和推理不一致等不可预测故障，导致可靠性不足，制约了其在生产环境中的广泛部署。

Method: 首先定义了故障类型分类体系，并提出定量可靠性评估模型；其次设计了基于执行模式和输出一致性的异常行为识别方法；最后开发了通过自适应重规划和纠正性提示实现动态恢复的自我修复机制。该框架在多智能体工作流环境中实现并通过真实任务场景进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法显著提高了任务成功率，减少了故障传播，增强了系统鲁棒性。特别之处在于建立了整合智能体内部推理过程与外部执行结果的监控体系。

Conclusion: 该框架为保障高级自主系统的稳定性提供了有效方案，并有望降低LLM在生产环境中的应用门槛。通过整合内部推理过程与外部执行结果的监控机制，为LLM智能体的可靠性保障提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Autonomous agents based on Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being utilized in complex software systems. However, reliability remains a significant challenge due to unpredictable failures such as hallucinations, execution errors, and inconsistent reasoning. This paper proposes a reliability-aware self-healing framework for LLM-based software agents. The framework integrates failure detection, reliability assessment, and automated recovery mechanisms. First, we define a taxonomy of failure types and introduce a quantitative reliability assessment model. Next, we propose a failure detection method that identifies abnormal agent behavior based on execution patterns and output consistency. Finally, we design a self-healing mechanism that dynamically recovers from failures through adaptive replanning and corrective prompting strategies. The proposed framework was implemented in a multi-agent workflow environment and evaluated using real-world task scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach significantly increases task success rates, reduces failure propagation, and enhances overall system robustness compared to existing methods. In particular, this study distinguishes itself by establishing an integrated monitoring system that combines the agent's internal reasoning process with external execution results. These findings are expected to contribute to securing the stability of advanced autonomous systems and lowering the barriers to LLM adoption in production environments.

</details>

*Advait Pavuluri,Bridget McGinn,Ashita Saxena,George Safta,Srikanth Tamilselvam,Raju Pavuluri,Michele Merler,Baishakhi Ray,Rahul Krishna*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Java remains central to enterprise software, and many applications outlive their original architecture. Migrating them across frameworks is a behavior-preserving refactoring spanning build configuration, dependency injection, persistence, request handling, and deployment. Existing software-engineering benchmarks cover bug fixing, feature implementation, and language or version modernization, but leave cross-framework refactoring largely unmeasured.
  We introduce ScarfBench, a benchmark for behavior-preserving cross-framework refactoring of enterprise Java applications. It is built from expert-written implementation triples across Spring, Jakarta EE, and Quarkus: 34 applications (29 focused single-layer, 5 whole) yielding 102 variants (~151K lines across 1946 source and test files) and 204 directed refactoring tasks. Each task gives an agent a working source application and a target framework; the agent must synthesize a target implementation preserving the source behavior. Correctness is evaluated by an application-specific executable oracle: the candidate must compile, deploy in a containerized target runtime, and pass behavioral tests over the application's observable interface.
  We evaluate five state-of-the-art coding agents on ScarfBench. The strongest achieves only 15.3% aggregate test pass on focused-layer migrations and 12.2% on whole applications, and only one of the 204 tasks yields a fully behaviorally equivalent target. Difficulty is asymmetric across framework directions and architectural layers: Spring<->Quarkus is the most tractable pair, and Jakarta-targeted migrations are hardest. From LLM-as-a-judge and expert adjudication of failed-task traces, we derive a taxonomy of recurring failure categories spanning build, deploy, and test stages. We release the benchmark, harness, and agent traces at https://scarfbench.info.

</details>


### [27] [Analyzing the Adoption of Database Management Systems Throughout the History of Open Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06817)
*Camila A. Paiva,Raquel Maximino,Frederico Paiva,Rafael Accetta Vieira,Nicole Espanha,João Felipe Pimentel,Igor Wiese,Marco Aurélio Gerosa,Igor Steinmacher,Leonardo Murta,Vanessa Braganholo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对362个GitHub开源Java项目进行历史分析，揭示了DBMS的采用趋势和演化模式，发现关系型MySQL/PostgreSQL主导、非关系型Redis/MongoDB稳定、多DBMS共存及ORM广泛使用等关键现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然先前研究已探讨了DBMS与应用源代码的协同演化，但对于DBMS在实际系统中的采用、共用和替换模式了解较少，缺乏系统的实证研究。

Method: 通过源代码启发式方法，分析了362个托管在GitHub上的流行开源Java项目，研究了DBMS的流行度、稳定性、迁移模式、共现模式以及ORM框架的作用。

Result: MySQL和PostgreSQL是最流行的DBMS；在非关系型DBMS中，Redis和MongoDB使用最频繁且稳定性高；HyperSQL等系统则更易被替换；多DBMS共用现象频繁，表明多语言持久化模式普遍存在；ORM框架广泛用于应用与DBMS之间的交互中介。

Conclusion: 本研究为开发者、架构师、教育者和DBMS供应商提供了关于DBMS采用、组合和替换的实证证据，有助于指导数据库技术的选型和演进决策。

Abstract: Database Management Systems (DBMSs) are widely used to store, retrieve, and manage the data handled by modern applications. Although prior work has studied the co-evolution of DBMSs and application source code, less is known about DBMS adoption, co-use, and replacement in real systems. This paper presents a historical study of DBMS usage in 362 popular open-source Java projects hosted on GitHub. We investigated the adoption of the top DBMSs ranked by DB-Engines, covering relational and non-relational systems. Using source-code heuristics, we analyzed DBMS popularity, stability, migration patterns, co-occurrence, and the role of Object-Relational Mappers (ORMs). Our findings show that MySQL and PostgreSQL are the most popular DBMSs in our corpus. Among non-relational DBMSs, Redis and MongoDB are the most frequently used and tend to remain stable after adoption. In contrast, systems such as HyperSQL are more often replaced as projects evolve. We also observed frequent co-use of multiple DBMSs, suggesting patterns of polyglot persistence in which projects combine systems to handle different data needs. Finally, we found that ORM frameworks are commonly used to mediate interactions between applications and DBMSs. Overall, our study provides empirical evidence on how DBMSs are adopted, combined, and replaced over time, offering guidance for developers, architects, educators, and DBMS vendors.

</details>


### [28] [Guidelines for Cultivating a Sense of Belonging to Reduce Developer Burnout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06827)
*Bianca Trinkenreich,Marco Aurelio Gerosa,Anita Sarma,Igor Steinmacher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文综述了软件开发和开源社区中的归属感研究，发现归属感特征包括信任、接纳、价值认可等，关联因素包括心理安全、内在动机等，并提出了包括及时认可、透明晋升规则、包容性福利、协作工具连接、无责事后分析等实用指南，以帮助减少开发者倦怠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业倦怠影响软件开发者的身心健康并导致人员流失，研究表明归属感缺失与更高倦怠水平相关，而归属感与韧性、工作满意度、参与度和幸福感正相关，因此需要为软件行业提供基于证据的归属感培养指导。

Method: 对软件开发和开源社区中的归属感研究进行综述，分析归属感的特征（如信任、接纳、价值认可、友谊、成员资格、相互支持、被了解）及其关联因素（包括认可、心理安全、内在动机、英语自信、任期、性别、文化权力距离），提出实用指南。

Result: 系统综述了归属感的特征（包括信任、接纳、价值认可、友谊、成员资格、相互支持、被了解）和关联因素（包括认可、心理安全、内在动机、英语自信、任期、性别、文化权力距离），并提出了一系列可操作的实践指南。

Conclusion: 提出了面向领导者和社区的实用指南，包括及时一致的认可、透明晋升规则、包容性福利和举措、通过协作工具建立有意联系、无责事后分析、可选线下聚会、非正式新人聚会、持续监测归属感和倦怠感。这些指南可帮助软件组织和开源社区培养更健康、更包容的环境，支持开发者福祉。

Abstract: Burnout affects software developers' mental and physical well-being and contributes to turnover, generating strong concerns in the software industry. Prior research has shown that lack of belonging is associated with higher levels of burnout among software developers, while a sense of belonging is linked to resilience, job satisfaction, engagement, and well-being. In this paper, we revisit recent studies on belongingness in software development teams, including proprietary software organizations and open-source software communities, to offer evidence-based guidelines for cultivating belongingness and reducing developer burnout. We summarize characteristics of belongingness, such as trust, acceptance, value recognition, friendship, membership, mutual support, and being known by others, as well as factors associated with belongingness, including recognition, psychological safety, intrinsic motivation, English confidence, tenure, gender, and cultural power distance. Based on these findings, we propose practical guidelines for leaders and communities, including timely and consistent recognition, transparent promotion rules, inclusive benefits and initiatives, intentional connections through collaborative tools, blameless postmortems, optional in-person opportunities, informal newcomer gatherings, and continuous monitoring of belongingness and burnout. These guidelines can help software organizations and open-source communities foster healthier, more inclusive environments that support developer well-being.

</details>


### [29] [SmellBench: Evaluating LLM Agents on Architectural Code Smell Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07001)
*Ion George Dinu,Marian Cristian Mihăescu,Traian Rebedea*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次对LLM智能体在架构代码异味修复任务上的系统性评估。最佳智能体在65个高严重度架构异味上达到47.7%修复率，但存在修复激进性与代码库质量负相关的悖论——最激进智能体引入140个新异味，揭示当前LLM在跨模块架构重构方面的能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 架构代码异味(architectural code smells)会侵蚀软件可维护性且修复成本高昂。与本地化Bug不同，架构异味需要跨模块推理设计意图，这对开发者和自动化工具都构成挑战。尽管大语言模型在Bug修复和代码级重构上表现出色，但尚未探索其在架构代码异味修复方面的能力。

Method: 构建SmellBench任务编排框架，包含：1)针对不同异味类型的优化提示策略；2)支持迭代多步执行的机制；3)分别评估修复效果、误报识别和净代码库影响的评分方法。评估来自4个模型家族(GPT、Claude、Gemini、Mistral)的11种智能体配置，在scikit-learn项目中由PyExamine检测的65个高严重度架构异味上进行测试，结果经专家判断验证。

Result: 专家验证发现63.1%的检测异味为误报；最佳智能体达到47.7%的解决率；智能体识别误报的专家一致性可达κ=0.94；但修复激进性与净代码库质量呈负相关，最激进的智能体反而引入140个新异味。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在本地化代码转换方面的能力与跨模块重构所需的架构理解之间存在显著差距。SmellBench提供了可复用的基础设施，可用于追踪自动化软件工程这一未充分探索领域的研究进展。代码和数据已开源(https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19247588)。

Abstract: Architectural code smells erode software maintainability and are costly to repair manually, yet unlike localized bugs, they require cross-module reasoning about design intent that challenges both developers and automated tools. While large language model agents excel at bug fixing and code-level refactoring, their ability to repair architectural code smells remains unexplored. We present the first empirical evaluation of LLM agents on architectural code smell repair. We contribute SmellBench, a task orchestration framework that incorporates smell-type-specific optimized prompts and supports iterative multi-step execution, together with a scoring methodology that separately evaluates repair effectiveness, false positive identification, and net codebase impact. We evaluate 11 agent configurations from four model families (GPT, Claude, Gemini, Mistral) on 65 hard-severity architectural smells detected by PyExamine in the Python project scikit-learn, validated against expert judgments. Expert validation reveals that 63.1% of detected smells are false positives, while the best agent achieves a 47.7% resolution rate. Agents identify false positives with up to $κ= 0.94$ expert agreement, but repair aggressiveness and net codebase quality are inversely related: the most aggressive agent introduces 140 new smells. These findings expose a gap between current LLM capabilities in localized code transformations and the architectural understanding needed for cross-module refactoring. SmellBench provides reusable infrastructure for tracking progress on this underexplored dimension of automated software engineering. We release our code and data at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19247588.

</details>


### [30] [SARC: A Governance-by-Architecture Framework for Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07728)
*Gaston Besanson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SARC是一个面向工具使用型AI智能体的运行时治理架构，将约束作为一等规范对象，通过四个执行站点（预行动门、行动时监控器、行动后审计器、升级路由器）在运行时强制执行，而非依赖提示词、仪表盘或事后文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI治理控制与AI执行之间存在结构性错位：在受监管环境中，必须约束执行的义务通常只在执行发生后才被评估。这种prompt-level或post-hoc的治理方式无法满足合规场景对实时约束的需求。

Method: SARC将约束声明为包含来源、类别、谓词、验证点、响应协议和操作点的规范对象，编译到智能体循环的四个执行点中；形式化规范-轨迹对应所需的最小不变量；证明有限奖励惩罚不能替代硬运行时约束；扩展到多智能体工作流（约束传播、权限交集、归因保留的轨迹树）。

Result: 在采购任务上对比5种基线方法：SARC在精确谓词下执行零次硬约束违反；声明的PAA节流响应使软窗口超限相对减少89.5%；谓词噪声和执行失败扫描表明残余硬违反源于执行栈错误而非环境机会。

Conclusion: SARC为义务的可执行、可检查、可审计提供了架构基础，使约束能够在运行时被强制执行而非事后评估。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems increasingly act through tools, sub-agents, and external services, but governance controls are still commonly attached to prompts, dashboards, or post-hoc documentation. This creates a structural mismatch in regulated settings: obligations that must constrain execution are often evaluated only after execution has occurred. We introduce SARC, a runtime governance architecture for tool-using agents that treats constraints as first-class specification objects alongside state, action space, and reward. A SARC specification declares each constraint's source, class, predicate, verification point, response protocol, and operating point, and compiles these into four enforcement sites in the agent loop: a Pre-Action Gate, an Action-Time Monitor, a Post-Action Auditor, and an Escalation Router. We formalize the minimal invariants required for specification-trace correspondence, show why finite reward penalties do not generally substitute for hard runtime constraints, and extend the architecture to multi-agent workflows through constraint propagation, authority intersection, and attribution-preserving trace trees. We implement a prototype audit checker and report a reproducible synthetic evaluation over 50 seeds comparing SARC against post-hoc audit, output filtering, workflow rules, and policy-as-code-only baselines on a procurement task. SARC executes zero hard-constraint violations under exact predicates; its declared PAA throttling response reduces soft-window overages by 89.5% relative to policy-as-code-only. Predicate-noise and enforcement-failure sweeps are consistent with the claim that residual hard violations under SARC scale with enforcement-stack error rather than environmental violation opportunity. SARC provides the architectural substrate through which obligations can be made executable, inspectable, and auditable at runtime.

</details>


### [31] [Low-code and no-code with BESSER to create and deploy smart web applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07376)
*Iván Alfonso,Armen Sulejmani,Aaron Conrardy,Jordi Cabot*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BESSER是一个开源的低代码框架，通过基于Web的编辑器支持应用程序的设计、生成和部署，旨在解决现有商业低代码平台带来的供应商锁定和扩展性受限等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着包含AI智能体的Web应用（智能Web应用）需求不断增长，传统的应用开发方式已难以满足快速交付的需求，低代码开发范式应运而生，旨在通过模型抽象和自动化代码生成减少手写代码量。

Method: 提出BESSER开源低代码框架，用户可通过基于Web的可视化编辑器设计应用模型，由框架自动生成应用程序代码并支持部署。框架基于模型驱动开发理念，将业务逻辑抽象为模型定义，结合代码生成器实现应用自动化构建。

Result: BESSER框架实现了应用程序的设计、生成与部署全流程支持，提供了透明且可扩展的低代码开发环境。

Conclusion: BESSER通过开源方式保证了平台的透明性和可扩展性，有效规避了商业低代码平台常见的供应商锁定风险、扩展性受限等问题，为开发者提供了自由可控的低代码解决方案。

Abstract: The increasing demand for web applications containing AI-agents, seen as smart web applications, has prompted the need for new techniques to facilitate their creation. Low-code has risen as an approach that reduces the amount of handwritten code by focusing on the abstraction of components in the form of models combined with automated generators to produce applications. Existing low-code platforms are commercial, leading to drawbacks such as the risk of vendor lock-in, limited extensibility, and more. We present the open-source BESSER low-code framework, which allows users to design, generate and deploy their application via a freely accessible web-based editor, while guaranteeing transparency and extensibility.

</details>


### [32] [Exploring CoCo Challenges in ML Engineering Teams: Insights From the Semiconductor Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07389)
*A. Azamnouri,M. Haug,L. Woltmann,M. Fritz,J. Bogner,S. Wagner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究在全球化半导体公司中对ML工程团队的协作与沟通（CoCo）挑战进行了定性调研，通过访谈12名从业者，识别出16个反复出现的挑战，其中角色和职责不清晰最为关键，并提出了缓解CoCo问题的有效实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习集成到复杂软件系统增加了团队协作与沟通的挑战。现有研究多聚焦于软件中心型企业，对硬件中心环境（如半导体行业）中CoCo挑战如何呈现缺乏实证理解。硬件中心环境具有严格数据治理、长开发周期、与物理过程紧密耦合等额外约束，这些约束会放大协调复杂性并降低灵活性。

Method: 在全球半导体公司中进行定性调研，访谈12名从业者，调查其CoCo实践、工具、挑战和方法。

Result: 识别出16个反复出现的CoCo挑战，其中"角色和职责不清晰"被确定为最关键挑战。同时归纳出从业者认为能有效缓解CoCo问题的常见实践和推荐方案。

Conclusion: 尽管研究基于单一组织背景，但发现与跨学科ML启用系统开发中的已知问题一致，同时揭示了这些挑战在硬件驱动约束下如何以不同方式呈现。结果为未来研究和工具支持指明了方向，以加强ML工程项目的CoCo并确保ML启用系统的成功。

Abstract: The integration of machine learning (ML) into complex software systems has increased challenges in collaboration and communication (CoCo) of the teams building these systems. ML engineering (MLE) teams often involve diverse roles, ML engineers, data scientists, software engineers, and domain experts, each bringing unique goals, experiences, and jargon. These interdisciplinary dynamics can make it challenging to deploy, reproduce, and maintain ML-enabled systems over the long term. Previous studies have uncovered several CoCo challenges and practices, but most have focused on software-centric companies, leaving limited empirical understanding of how these dynamics unfold in hardware-centric contexts. In hardware-centric environments, CoCo challenges are shaped by additional constraints such as strict data governance, long development cycles, and tight coupling with physical processes, which amplify coordination complexity and reduce flexibility. To strengthen empirical understanding in such settings, we present a qualitative investigation of MLE teams within a global semiconductor company, where ML-enabled systems and manufacturing processes introduce additional complexity. We interviewed 12 practitioners regarding CoCo practices, tools, challenges, and approaches. Through analysis, we identified 16 recurring challenges, with unclear roles and responsibilities emerging as the most critical, and common practices and recommendations practitioners considered effective in mitigating CoCo problems. While grounded in a single organizational context, our findings align with known issues in interdisciplinary ML-enabled systems development, but also demonstrate how these challenges manifest differently under hardware-driven constraints. Our results highlight directions for future research and tool support to strengthen CoCo in MLE projects and ensure the success of ML-enabled systems.

</details>


### [33] [Boosting Automatic Java-to-Cangjie Translation with Multi-Stage LLM Training and Error Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07403)
*Xinyue Liang,Jingxuan Zhang,Lin Li,Jun Zhang,Junhao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个多阶段训练框架，利用迭代错误修复技术将Java代码翻译为Cangjie代码，解决了低资源编程语言翻译中语法错误和语义对齐的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Cangjie新兴编程语言的崛起，对其生态系统的需求增长，但现有大型语言模型受限于Cangjie知识不足和并行语料稀缺，在代码翻译中容易出现语法错误和语义结构错位。现有方法依赖大规模并行数据微调，无法可靠提升低资源语言的编译性和语义一致性。

Method: 提出了一个多阶段训练框架，包括：1) 构建句法知识和单语指令数据集训练LLM；2) 在训练过程中逐步整合知识，实现语义对齐和结构感知；3) 结合编译器反馈和错误修复案例检索来修复错误的Cangjie代码；4) 构建Cangjie错误修复库支持错误修复流程。

Result: 实验结果表明，在有限并行数据条件下，该方法相比现有最优方法将功能等价性提升了6.06%。消融实验验证了每个训练阶段都对最终性能有正向贡献。

Conclusion: 该多阶段训练框架结合迭代错误修复技术能够有效提升低资源编程语言的代码翻译质量，为新兴编程语言的生态建设提供了可行的自动化翻译方案。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of emerging programming language ecosystems, the demand for code translation to low-resource languages continues to grow. As Cangjie emerges as a new programming language, its ecosystem and development toolchains are rapidly expanding. Automated translation from popular programming languages to Cangjie is therefore valuable for practical development. However, constrained by both insufficient Cangjie knowledge and scarce parallel code corpora, general Large Language Models (LLMs) are prone to syntactic errors and semantic as well as structural misalignment in code translation. Existing approaches typically rely on fine-tuning with large-scale parallel data, but they cannot reliably improve compilability or semantic consistency for low-resource Cangjie languages. To tackle these challenges, we propose a multi-stage training framework of LLMs that employs the iterative error repair technique to translate Java code into Cangjie code. This training framework performs training on LLMs, gradually integrating knowledge and achieving semantic alignment as well as structure awareness. During the code translation, we also combine the compiler feedback and error repair case retrieval to repair the incorrect Cangjie code. We construct syntactic knowledge and monolingual instruction datasets to train the LLM. In addition, we also build a Cangjie error repair repository to support error repair in our approach. Experimental results show that, with limited parallel data, our approach improves functional equivalence by 6.06\% compared to the state-of-the-art approaches. Meanwhile, ablation studies confirm that each training stage positively contributes to the final performance.

</details>


### [34] [Prompt Engineering Strategies for LLM-based Qualitative Coding of Psychological Safety in Software Engineering Communities: A Controlled Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07422)
*Moaath Alshaikh,Tasneem Alshaher,Ricardo Vieira,Beatriz Santana,Clelio Xavier,Jose Amancio,Glauco Carneiro,Julio Leite,Savio Freire,Manoel Mendonca*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控实验评估了三种LLM（Claude Haiku、DeepSeek-Chat、Gemini 2.5 Flash）在定性编码任务中的表现，发现多样本提示策略可显著提升部分模型的编码一致性，但模型间稳定性差异显著，且存在系统性预测偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定性分析在软件工程研究中至关重要，但传统人工编码过程耗时且易受主观因素影响。LLM虽有望辅助该过程，但其可靠性尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 采用受控实验设计，评估三种LLM在零样本和多样本两种提示策略下的表现，使用Cohen's kappa作为一致性指标，每种配置进行10次独立运行。

Result: 多样本提示显著提升Claude Haiku的一致性（Delta kappa = +0.034, p = 0.004），但对DeepSeek-Chat和Gemini 2.5 Flash无显著影响。模型稳定性差异明显：DeepSeek-Chat和Claude Haiku最稳定（SD ≈ 0.017），Gemini 2.5 Flash最不稳定（SD = 0.038）。所有模型均存在系统性过度预测"分享负面反馈"（偏差比高达5.25倍）和低估"表达担忧"的倾向。

Conclusion: 该研究为LLM辅助定性编码的提示工程提供了实证依据，表明多样本策略的效果因模型而异，且需关注系统性预测偏差问题。

Abstract: Qualitative analysis plays a pivotal role in understanding the human and social aspects of software engineering. However, it remains a demanding process shaped by the subjective interpretation of individual researchers and sensitive to methodological choices such as prompt design. Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) offer promising opportunities to support this type of analysis, although their reliability in reproducing human qualitative reasoning under varying prompting conditions remains largely untested. This study presents a controlled empirical evaluation of three LLMs -- Claude Haiku, DeepSeek-Chat, and Gemini 2.5 Flash -- across two prompt engineering strategies (zero-shot and multi-shot closed coding), using Cohen's kappa as the primary agreement metric over ten independent runs per configuration. Results suggest that multi-shot prompting significantly improves agreement for Claude Haiku (Delta kappa = +0.034, Wilcoxon p = 0.004) but not for DeepSeek-Chat or Gemini 2.5 Flash. Intra-model stability varies substantially -- DeepSeek-Chat and Claude Haiku exhibit the lowest variance (SD approx. 0.017), while Gemini 2.5 Flash is the least stable (SD = 0.038). A systematic over-prediction of "Sharing Negative Feedback" is identified across all models (bias ratios up to 5.25x), alongside consistent under-prediction of "Expressing Concerns." Collectively, these findings provide empirical evidence for prompt engineering guidelines in LLM-assisted qualitative coding for software engineering research.

</details>
